| A-210 | 2026-11-16 | **PAY's provider port, webhooks, allocations and refunds as built in B09a continued (FR-PAY-003 to FR-PAY-010, FR-PAY-012, FR-PAY-013, ADR-0063), each the most conservative reading, changeable as data or by a forward migration.** <ul><li>**(a) Paystack's sandbox is not used:** it needs a test secret key this repository never holds, so the adapter is tested against a local fake of Paystack's documented API (`test/support/fake-paystack.ts`); no live endpoint is ever called, and a live key (`sk_live_`) is refused by configuration outside production. Unconfigured, every provider call is unavailable (503) and cash still works. Paystack's own names are verified against its documentation only (A-064 stays open for the sandbox run, before go-live): mobile money by `POST /charge` with `mobile_money.provider` `mtn`, `vod` (Telecel) and `atl` (AirtelTigo); `GET /transaction/verify`; `GET /transaction` with `from`, `to`, `page`, `perPage`; `POST /refund` and `GET /refund/:id`. Paystack requires a payer email: the farm's own mailbox (`PAYSTACK_PAYER_EMAIL`) is sent, never a customer's.</li><li>**(b) Webhook order (the owner, 2026-11-16):** the signature over the raw body first (constant time, 401 with no detail); then the event's own time within 300 seconds either side (`pay.webhook_window_seconds`; Paystack signs no separate timestamp, so the signed `paid_at` is used, stricter than API.md's retry-horizon option); then once by the provider's reference: the event id is `<event>:<reference>`, unique among events that reached processing (accepted, rejected on amount, unknown reference, lookup deferred), so a stale or forged delivery never blocks the genuine one; only then the server-to-server lookup. A payload amount that differs from the lookup is `REJECTED_AMOUNT` and changes nothing; a lookup amount that differs from what was asked holds the payment PENDING with a `PROVIDER_AMOUNT_DIFFERS` break. A lookup that cannot be made is `DEFERRED_LOOKUP` and the sweep clears it. Every signed event is answered 200.</li><li>**(c) One clearing path:** `ProviderPayments.settleIn`, from the webhook, `checkPaymentStatus` or the sweep; the database refuses a mobile money or card payment cleared without its charge SUCCEEDED for exactly its amount (`trg_payment_provider_confirmed`). A provider payment clears as an unallocated customer deposit (Dr 1120 against the provider, Cr 2200 against the customer or the walk-in party) for the order's document to be allocated later; money paid after the 15-minute window, or after the order released the charge (`releaseChargeIn`, the port ORD's cancellation calls in B09a continued 2), clears the same way with an `UNMATCHED_PAYMENT` break and `heldAsUnmatched` on the event, never lost and never the order's. No refund is requested automatically: a person decides (the SRS's acceptance; ARCHITECTURE section 7.2's "a refund is requested" waits for them).</li><li>**(d) The provider sweep (the owner, 2026-11-16):** the `provider-sweep` job every minute reads Paystack's transaction list from where the last run ended (`pay.provider_sweep`, one row per run; a run that cannot reach the provider records nothing and the next reads the same window), applies every transaction of ours through `settleIn`, raises a success under a reference we never initiated as `PROVIDER_REFERENCE_UNKNOWN` (nothing posted), and looks up every charge still open 30 seconds after it was sent; a charge the provider has no record of 30 minutes after it was sent fails with a `PROVIDER_PAYMENT_MISSING` break. Each break is raised once while open and published as `pay.ReconciliationBreakRaised` for the daily exception report (B12).</li><li>**(e) The port's resilience:** a 10-second timeout, three attempts with full jitter (200 milliseconds doubling, capped at 2 seconds) and a circuit breaker (five failures in a row open it for 30 seconds). A charge retried after a timeout reuses our reference, which Paystack refuses as a duplicate: that answer is read as PENDING, so the customer is charged once. The breaker is per process.</li><li>**(f) Initiation:** the payment is written PENDING (`UNVERIFIED`) with our reference `HK-<payment id>` before the provider hears of it; the payer's phone is stored masked only. Without `orderId` it is taken at the caller's one branch (else `BRANCH_REQUIRED`); `orderId` names a registered sales order; `shiftId` is refused until B9b. The 201 answer is stored with the key; a replay answers the payment as it is now and sends nothing more.</li><li>**(g) Allocation:** only a cleared payment, to issued invoices, receipts or debit notes of the same customer (or both walk-in) at its branch; ◆ `pay.DepositAllocated` carries `applied` (Dr 2200 Cr 1200) or `unapplied` (Dr 1200 Cr 2200, an allocation taken off with a reason, once). Allocations made at clearing (a counter sale) stay in `pay.PaymentCleared` as B9a built them.</li><li>**(h) Receipt links (the owner's A-208 (f) condition):** a counter sale's payments are allocated in full to its numbered receipt at once; a mobile money deposit is linked to a numbered document only when it is allocated, and until then through its order (`SO-...`, numbered when ORD places it) or, without one, only by its customer and its listing (`listPayments?unallocatedOnly=true`); this is said plainly in the report.</li><li>**(i) Refunds (the owner, 2026-11-16):** every refund a person asks for waits for a different holder of `pay.refund.approve` with a fresh step-up (`REFUND` policy threshold 0, data), so a single Owner cannot refund alone; the SRS's automatic limit of 20,000 applies only to the automatic catch-weight refunds of B11. A refund draws only on the payment's unallocated money: refunds not rejected never exceed the amount less what is allocated (`trg_refund_cap` on `refunded_pesewas`; a rejection gives it back). Approved, a mobile money or card refund to the original method goes through the provider (`refund-dispatch`, every minute; the refund's id its idempotency key; retried after 5 and 10 minutes, and after the third failure FAILED with a `REFUND_FAILED` break); any other waits for `markRefundPaid`, which needs the payout's reference, a fresh step-up and someone other than the maker (`ck_refund_paid_by`). Paid, ◆ `pay.RefundPaid` posts Dr 2200 Cr the original tender's account. Refunds are unnumbered business documents (`REFUND`). Store credit and a credit note's refund (Dr 1200) need B09a continued 1b.</li><li>**(j) Grants:** read payments and refunds: Owner, Manager, Accountant, Sales, Cashier; initiate mobile money: Owner, Manager, Cashier, Sales (the driver with B11); allocate: Owner, Accountant; ask for a refund: Owner, Manager, Cashier, Sales; approve: Owner, Manager, Accountant; record a payout: Owner, Manager, Accountant.</li></ul> | Owner (b, i, j), Accountant (c, g, i) | B09a continued | **Approved by the owner, 2026-11-16** |

(5) The transaction list is newest first and an empty list has `meta.pageCount` 0, not 1. (6) A refund beyond what is left is 400 `Total refund amount cannot exceed original transaction amount`; a refund of an unpaid (abandoned) transaction is 400 `This transaction failed`. It cannot be confirmed (422 PRICE_OVERRIDE_NEEDS_APPROVAL, `APPROVAL_PENDING` or `APPROVAL_REJECTED`) and its proforma cannot be issued until approved. A smaller payment taken against the order (by a cashier) leaves it waiting. Staff-entered PREPAID orders are still confirmed by hand.</li><li>**(e) Stopping:** a PLACED online order is cancelled as `PAYMENT_NOT_RECEIVED` when its checkout's hold expires or is released first, or its charge fails; its pending charge is released, so money paid later clears as an unmatched deposit with a break for a person to refund (A-210 (c)). A failed charge and a staff cancellation also release the checkout's stock, so the cart is ACTIVE again at once; an expiry leaves the rest of the checkout's stock to the expiry job.</li><li>**(f) Tracking:** `trackOrder` is anonymous by a token `trk_` + base64url(order id bytes + 128-bit HMAC-SHA256 under a key derived from `LOOKUP_HMAC_KEY` for this purpose), never stored; it shows the number, status, steps, totals and line names only. `delivery` is null until B11's deliveries. A reversal carries its original's value; a value-only addition, a gain, a return or a reversal that leaves the pool off its schedule re-anchors it at what it then holds at the next issue. Everything that is not a bought lot keeps ADR-0018's rule. | A-272 | 2026-11-17 | **The claim from a receipt's rejected lines as built (B09e continued 3, the owner's decision 5, CR-036, ADR-0076 proposed; migrations 0222, 0223), each the most conservative reading.** (a) **Settled once:** by the approved invoice of the order that left the rejected goods out (`settleSupplierClaim`, refused `CLAIM_INVOICED` if any live invoice holds more of an order line than was received and other claims' credit notes cover), or by the credit note drafted from it against the approved invoice that charged them (`draftSupplierClaimCreditNote`, refused `CLAIM_NOT_INVOICED` otherwise), settled when that credit note is approved; the three-way match lets an invoice carry exactly the rejected quantity of claims not settled by an invoice, and the approved invoice's `orderValuePesewas` for such a line is its received part only, so the rejected value waits on 5140 until the credit note clears it (no posting rule, account or FIN code changed). (b) **The value:** the delivered base quantity at the order line's price through the order line's own `qty_ordered / qty_ordered_base`, rounded half-up once, then `allocate` with keys accepted (1) and rejected (2), so the accepted share is `half_up(line x accepted / delivered)` (ADR-0075's half-up) and the claim the rest; no request carries an amount (strict body, 400). (c) **The credit note's lines:** one per claim line in the product's base unit, quantity the rejected base quantity, net the claimed value, unit price 0 (a share of the delivery, not a quantity times a price), held by a composite foreign key to the claim line; the `round(qty x price)` check is lifted for claim lines only; a claim's credit note is never changed (`CLAIM_CREDIT_NOTE_FIXED`: cancel and draft again); one not cancelled per claim. (d) **Maker and checker:** besides the credit note's maker and submitter, the claim's maker and whoever posted the receipt are refused as its approver (`INVOICE_MAKER_CANNOT_APPROVE`), in the handler and by `pur.supplier_claim_guard` when the claim is settled. (e) **Permissions:** no new permission: raising and drafting take `pur.supplier_invoice.create` (Owner, Accountant), settling by an invoice `pur.supplier_invoice.match`, reading `pur.supplier_invoice.read` (Owner, Manager, Accountant); the Storekeeper who receives is refused. (f) **The receipt answers its claims** through `listGoodsReceiptClaims` (`GET /goods-receipts/{id}/claims`, PUR's), not a field on `GoodsReceipt`, since INV reads nothing of PUR; INV publishes `GoodsReceipts.factsIn` for the claim. The witness's PIN on the device is B09b continued 3.</li><li>**(e) Postings (migration 0228):** the float Dr 1000 (the register) Cr 1010; a drop Dr 1010 Cr 1000; a pay-in Dr 1000 Cr 1010; a pay-out Dr the expense its reason determines (`EXPENSE_CATEGORY`, 6800 by default) Cr 1000; the sign-off Cr 1000 expected, Dr 1010 declared, Dr 6900 short or Cr 6900 over. Each once per subject (`uq_journal_entry_pos_subject`).</li><li>**(f) Grants (migration 0230):** the Cashier, the Manager and the Owner open a shift, move cash, declare their own count and sell; only the Owner and the Manager sign a count off, with a fresh step-up, and never their own; the Owner and the Manager set tills up; the Owner, Manager, Accountant and Cashier read tills, shifts and reports, a cashier only their own. Recording cash on another cashier's shift is left to `pos.shift.force_close` (OFFLINE_SYNC 6.5), which is seeded with the force close.</li><li>**(g) Times:** an online shift opens, and cash moves, at the application clock's time; the time the till sends must be within 300 seconds of it (`sync.clock_tolerance_seconds`' default, a constant until the setting is seeded in B09b continued 2).</li><li>**(h) The seeded day:** till T1 is set up by the seeded manager and the seeded cashier's shift on it is open with no float, because the seeded books hold no cash in the safe to give one; every seeded sale's cash is in that shift. The yams sale is on channel POS; the goat sale stays a wholesale sale at the counter, so its wholesale prices and the seeded figures hold.</li><li>**(i) Expected cash** counts the shift's CLEARED cash payments less what was refunded of them, whenever the refund was paid. A cash refund paid out of a till outside its open shift is not yet refused (`markRefundPaid`, PAY): B09b continued refuses it, as A-212 (a) refuses cash coming in.</li><li>**(j) Reports:** an X report is a reading and is not stored; the Z report is stored once at the sign-off. Price given below list counts the lines sold below their resolved price on completed sales of the shift.</li><li>**(k) Cash into a till names its shift (migration 0227):** PAY refuses a cash tender into a till with no shift (`SHIFT_REQUIRED`), and so does the database (`ck_payment_cash_shift`). That the named document is a SHIFT, open and the seller's own is POS's to check under the shift's lock, because PAY sits below POS and reads neither POS's tables nor the platform's registry.</li></ul> | Owner (a, c, d, f, h), Accountant (e, i) | B9b | **Owner, 2026-11-17:** (d) changed: a supervisor witnesses a drop when one is signed in at the branch; with none, the drop is allowed, marked unwitnessed and put on the exception report (built in B09b continued). (f) approved: the Owner holds the till permissions and is still never the checker of their own action. (g) approved at 300 seconds: a sale outside it is recorded and flagged, never refused (B09b continued 2). (h), (i) and (j) approved. The rest Open |
| A-274 | 2026-11-17 | **The till as built in B09b continued (FR-POS-001 to FR-POS-006, FR-POS-010, FR-POS-016, ADR-0016, ADR-0077; migrations 0231 to 0235), each the most conservative reading, changeable without a contract change unless it says so.** <ul><li>**(a) The split (ADR-0026 Amendments).** Built: device enrolment, the counter sale, the till cash rule on every PAY path, the cash refund out of an open shift, the unwitnessed drop. The force close, held sales, a cleared provider payment as a counter tender and the audit anchor at each sign-off are B09b continued 1b.</li><li>**(b) The device credential** is `hkdev_<till code>.` and 256 random bits (base64url), stored only as its SHA-256 hash; it is kept in no idempotency answer, audit row or outbox payload, so a replayed `enrolDevice` answers 409 `DEVICE_CREDENTIAL_SHOWN_ONCE` and the manager revokes and enrols again. Enrolling and revoking need `pos.device.enrol` with a fresh step-up; only the Owner and the Manager hold it and `pos.device.read` (migrations 0233, 0234). A revoked device is never un-revoked (`pos.device_enrolment_guard`).</li><li>**(c) Where the device is checked:** on `completeCounterSale` (and the sync, B09b continued 2). `openShift` and `recordCashMovement` stay on the staff session as B9b built them (BL-078).</li><li>**(d) Recovering a till:** `pos.shift.force_close` (step-up) is seeded now to the Owner and the Manager, because ADR-0077's check lets its holder record cash, pay a refund or approve a reversal on another cashier's open shift; the force close operation itself is B09b continued 1b. Nobody, a manager included, sells on another cashier's shift.</li><li>**(e) A cash refund** of a payment taken at a till is paid only out of an open shift of that till, named on `markRefundPaid` (`shiftId`), and counts against that shift's expected cash; a payment whose shift has closed is refunded only after a new shift opens on that till, or by the accountant's approved manual journal. A mobile money or card tender is refused (`PAYMENT_METHOD_REQUIRES_VERIFICATION`); a tender naming a cleared provider payment (`paymentId`) is refused `PROVIDER_PAYMENT_NOT_AVAILABLE`, because a cleared payment is frozen and ORD reads an order's payments by the order they name: linking it to the sale needs a decision (B09b continued 1b). | A-275 | 2026-11-17 | **The force close and the anchor at each sign-off as built in B09b continued 1b (FR-POS-020, ADR-0078, ADR-0006 Amendments; migrations 0245 and 0246), each the most conservative reading, changeable without a contract change unless it says so.** <ul><li>**(a) The split (ADR-0026 Amendments).** Built: `forceCloseShift`, the later count of its drawer (`countForceClosedShift`, new), ADR-0077's force-closed column proved with the real operation, the audit anchor at each sign-off. Held sales and a cleared provider payment as a counter tender are **B09b continued 1c**.</li><li>**(b) Only an OPEN shift is force-closed.** A CLOSING shift already has its cashier's declared count, which a supervisor signs off (`approveShiftClose`); forcing it would throw the count away. 422 `SHIFT_NOT_OPEN`.</li><li>**(c) A force close posts nothing.** The shift is CLOSED at the cash its records say the drawer holds (`expected_cash_pesewas`, frozen), with no declared count, and the till's 1000 keeps that cash until the drawer is counted, because nobody has counted it. In a branch with one manager, the Owner counts.</li><li>**(e) The anchor at each sign-off** is asked for in the sign-off's own transaction (`platform.audit_anchor_request`, with that transaction's id) and answered by the worker as the sealer once every audit event of that transaction is in the chain (`platform.audit_anchor_fulfilment`, one per request); one anchor may answer several requests. | A-276 | 2026-11-17 | **Held sales and the counter's provider tender as built in B09b continued 1c (FR-POS-006, FR-POS-007, ADR-0079; migrations 0250 and 0251; contract 2.10.0), each the most conservative reading, changeable without a contract change unless it says so.** <ul><li>**(a) A held sale reserves nothing.** FR-POS-007 allows a setting that reserves held animals; none is built, so a hold has nothing to release. An animal on a held sale can be sold meanwhile; the counter sale that completes the recalled sale re-checks stock and refuses it then.</li><li>**(b) A hold lapses with its shift:** at the cashier's declaration (`closeShift`: nothing more is sold on a CLOSING shift) and at a force close, in that transaction, each lapse audited; the database refuses a shift leaving OPEN while a sale is still held on it (`ck_shift_held_sales_lapsed`).</li><li>**(c) A hold lives at most twelve hours** (`HELD_SALE_LIFETIME_MS`), its `expiresAt`, if its shift runs that long: the column needs a bound when the shift's end is unknown, and a sale parked longer is not one the customer is coming back for.</li><li>**(d) Who holds and recalls:** only the cashier of the till's open shift, on that shift, from a device of that till. With one open shift per till, FR-POS-007's "any cashier with the open shift on that register" is always that shift's cashier while the hold lives; a manager neither holds nor recalls, because nobody sells on another cashier's shift (A-274 (d), ADR-0077). Anyone who sells at the till's location may list its holds.</li><li>**(e) What is held:** the lines as a counter sale carries them, without prices or tenders, and the customer if named. A hold is refused if a line has no price to sell at now (`HELD_SALE_INVALID` `QUOTE_REQUIRED`, `WEIGHT_REQUIRED`): it could never be sold. At recall a line that no longer resolves shows a null price and the total is null; the recall still succeeds.</li><li>**(f) A recall keeps the row** (RECALLED, by whom and when) rather than deleting it, so the recall is audited and a second one is 404; `recallHeldSale` stays `DELETE` as planned.</li><li>**(g) The provider tender is the whole payment:** `amountPesewas` must equal the cleared payment's amount (`AMOUNT_DIFFERS`), so nothing of it is left on deposit and no change is ever given from it. A walk-in sale takes only a payment for nobody, a named customer's sale only that customer's (`OTHER_CUSTOMER` both ways). A payment of another branch is answered `PAYMENT_UNKNOWN`, as an id that names no payment.</li><li>**(h) Only a payment taken at the sale's own shift:** a charge initiated with no shift (away from the till) or at another shift is `OTHER_SHIFT`, so every mobile money tender is counted in the shift that sold it.</li><li>**(i) A void of a sale paid by mobile money or card is refused** (`TENDER_NOT_VOIDABLE`, ORD's existing rule, which now sees the linked payment); the money goes back through a return's refund to the original method.</li><li>**(j) New `HeldSale` fields** `shiftId`, `status`, `heldTotalPesewas`, `pricing` and `currency` are optional in the contract and always sent, so no existing example had to change shape.</li><li>**(k) The seed is not extended:** a held sale is a cashier's work in progress that lapses with its shift; the seeded day leaves T1's shift open, and a parked sale seeded onto it would be a sale nobody is coming back for. | A-277 | 2026-11-18 | **The offline pack, the offline settings and a counter line in its own unit as built in B09b continued 2 (FR-POS-010, OFFLINE_SYNC 5.1 and 5.2, CR-031, CR-032 point 6, CR-038; contract 2.11.0; no migration), each the most conservative reading, changeable without a contract change unless it says so.** <ul><li>**(a) The split (ADR-0026 Amendments, accepted by the parent session on 2026-11-18).** Built: the settings of OFFLINE_SYNC 5.2 seeded, `getOfflinePack` with CR-031, CR-032 point 6 and CR-038's `sellingUnits`, and CR-038 at the online counter (`CounterSaleLine.uom`). Offline sync (`syncOfflineSales`, `pos.sale_sync`, ADR-0027's five conflict tests, the 500-sale test, `Billing.issueIn` at `occurred_at` with the age bound, the 300-second flag at sync, the shift rules offline, CR-038 at sync) is **B09b continued 2b**, inserted before B09b continued 3.</li><li>**(b) The settings' bounds are their documented defaults** (`pos.offline.max_hours` 1 to 8, `pos.offline.max_queued_sales` 1 to 500, `sync.clock_tolerance_seconds` 30 to 300, `pay.unverified_window_days` 1 to 3; `pos.offline.allow_unverified_mobile_money` a boolean, false): the owner can make a till stricter without a deployment, never looser. The three offline keys are per branch, the other two farm-wide. Each is seeded farm-wide at its default by the seeded Owner through AUD's audited administration.</li><li>**(c) Who reads the pack:** a holder of `pos.sale.create` at the till's branch and location, from an enrolled device of that till that is not revoked. No open shift is needed, because the device uses the pack to open a shift offline (OFFLINE_SYNC 10); the cashier's session and the device are both still required.</li><li>**(d) What the pack prices:** the channel POS walk for a buyer with no account (the till's customer is unknown until the sale), for every product that is ACTIVE, held in stock and priced by a list in force; `validUntil` is `generatedAt` plus `pos.offline.max_hours`, not shortened to a price change scheduled inside that window (the server re-prices every synced sale at its own time).</li><li>**(e) The units a product is sold by at a till** are those some list in force prices it in, that it converts to its base unit: the sale unit first when it is priced in it, the rest by code. A unit it converts but no list prices is not sold (a crate of cabbage, 24 heads, is never priced as 24 times a head); a meat pack priced by the kilogram is sold by the kilogram. A line that names no unit is in the first; a unit not sold is 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED` `UNIT_NOT_SOLD`.</li><li>**(f) A unit's band** is what the engine resolves for one of it with no grade and no weight and no quantity break. A product priced by grade or weight (a live animal) therefore has no single band in the pack (`priceBand` null, `quoteOnly` false); `quoteOnly` is true only for an entry marked quote only. A product's band, entry, basis and `taxInclusive` are its first unit's (`taxInclusive` from the list that unit is priced from). Pricing an animal offline from its grade and last weight is BL-080.</li><li>**(g) The reasons:** every `PRICE_OVERRIDE` reason and the `CASH_MOVEMENT` reasons `recordCashMovement` takes (A-273 (c)); no `VOID` reason, because no sale is voided offline (OFFLINE_SYNC 2).</li><li>**(h) Offline tenders:** the methods allowed offline other than mobile money, and mobile money as well only while `pos.offline.allow_unverified_mobile_money` is on for the branch (both must be on, OFFLINE_SYNC 5.2). The unverified tender itself is taken at sync (B09b continued 2b).</li><li>**(i) The override limits** in the policy are the caller's role limits at the branch (`PRICE_OVERRIDE_PER_LINE`, `PRICE_OVERRIDE_PER_SHIFT`), not what the shift has left; none is seeded (A-230), so both are 0 and the device allows no price below list offline until the owner sets limits.</li><li>**(j) The stock units** are every animal and lot with stock on hand at the till's location, `sellable` when none of it is reserved beyond it, no hold covers it and it is not past its expiry date; with its grade and last weight and never a cost. Countable stock with no unit is not listed (the device never stops a countable sale on a cached quantity, OFFLINE_SYNC 5.1).</li><li>**(k) The clock tolerance** is read from `sync.clock_tolerance_seconds` by `openShift`, `recordCashMovement` and `completeCounterSale` (A-273 (g)'s constant is its default), reused for up to 30 seconds after a change as every setting is.</li><li>**(l) A counter line that names no unit (LOUD in 2.11.0)** is in the first unit the product is sold by, as `completeCounterSale` always documented (eggs by the crate); the code before sold it in the base unit (one egg). A held sale's line names no unit, so it is priced the same way; a unit on a held line is BL-081.</li><li>**(m) Each issue of the pack is audited** (`ISSUE_OFFLINE_PACK` on the register: the device, the versions, the counts), so the server can tell what a synced sale was priced from.</li></ul> | Owner (a, b, c, d, e, f, h, i, l), Accountant (f, i) | B09b continued 2b | Open |
| A-278 | 2026-11-18 | **Offline sync as built in B09b continued 2b (FR-POS-011 to FR-POS-014, ADR-0013, ADR-0015, ADR-0027, ADR-0077; migrations 0256 and 0257; contract 2.13.0), each the most conservative reading, changeable without a contract change unless it says so.** <ul><li>**(a) The split (ADR-0026 Amendments).** Built: `syncOfflineSales` with `pos.sale_sync`, exactly once by client UUIDv7, the device counter and its gaps, provisional references, both clocks, the receipt issued at `occurred_at` and marked synced, the age bound, the clock flag, ADR-0077 offline, CR-038 at sync, CR-031's `grossPesewas`, ADR-0027's five conflict tests and the 500-sale test. **B09b continued 2c**, inserted before B09b continued 3: a shift opened offline and synced later (`openShift` refuses an `openedAt` outside the clock tolerance), the cash movements of a batch (answered `REJECTED` with reason `CASH_MOVEMENT_NOT_TAKEN_AT_SYNC` until then, so the till keeps them to record online), and the supervisor-approved unverified mobile money tender recorded `UNVERIFIED` with its `UNVERIFIED_PAYMENT` break (OFFLINE_SYNC 9.9; until then it is not recorded and raises `UNMATCHED_PAYMENT`, REVIEW).</li><li>**(b) Posted late lives in `pos.sale_sync`** (`posted_late_reason`, `posting_date`), not on `ord.sales_order` as OFFLINE_SYNC 13 proposed: no ORD migration (the parent session for lane A, 2026-11-18).</li><li>**(c) How old an offline sale may be:** `sync.max_sale_age_hours`, default 8 (the pack's own validity), bounded 1 to 8 so it can only be made stricter, farm-wide, in AUD's catalogue and read at its default until the owner sets it (not seeded). A sale older than the bound when it reaches the server is recorded and flagged `OFFLINE_SALE_TOO_OLD` (REVIEW), never refused.</li><li>**(d) The synced receipt (the owner's addition (1) to A-200):** `Billing.issueIn` takes `syncedOffline: { issueDate }` for a synced sale only. `issued_at` is the till's `occurred_at`; `issue_date` is the business date it happened on, or the date FIN posts it on when that date's period is no longer open (`Ledger.postingDateIn`, the recorded date, ADR-0027 point 6); its levies are the tax scheme in force when it happened; its number and the order's take that date's year; `synced_from_offline` is true (migration 0257), shown by the document read. The provisional reference is stored on the receipt when it is the one the shift and counter make (else (j)).</li><li>**(e) [Approved by the owner on 2026-11-18 as amended: cash from the queue whose shift has closed, is closing or was force-closed is recorded on that shift as a late entry, never "recorded nowhere"; the text below is the reading it replaces; built in B09b continued 3b, ADR-0077 Amendments, A-281] LOUD, for the owner: cash from the queue goes only onto its own shift while that shift is OPEN** (ADR-0077 offline). Where ADR-0027 principle 2 ("payment posts") meets ADR-0077 (no cash into a closed, closing or force-closed shift), ADR-0077 holds, as the opener says: the sale and its receipt are recorded, **no cash is recorded on any shift**, the receipt stays outstanding on 1200, and `OFFLINE_CASH_NOT_TAKEN` (URGENT) carries the cash the till claimed. One function decides it (`tillTakesCash` in `src/modules/pos/domain/offline-sync.ts`), so the owner can rule otherwise in one place. The sale is also `OFFLINE_LATE_POSTING`: `SHIFT_CLOSED` (REVIEW) or, dated after a force close, `AFTER_FORCE_CLOSE` (URGENT); it posts on its own date while that period is open (OFFLINE_SYNC 9.4 says the first open date; with no cash on the shift nothing frozen is touched, so its own date is kept). The parent session accepted this reading and puts it to the owner.</li><li>**(f) [Open; the owner ruled on 2026-11-18 that an offline sale may take a location below zero, valued at the last known cost, built in B09b continued 3b] LOUD: stock gone negative where the location does not allow it.** A `SALE_ISSUE_OFFLINE` goes below zero only at a location with `allows_negative` (migration 0044). The seeded shop floor does not allow it, so there a line beyond the stock is recorded with no movement and a `NEGATIVE_STOCK` exception for its whole quantity in the movement's place (UAT 6.13 and 6.14 expect "minus 3 crates"; the stock stays at what it was until the exception is resolved). A lot-tracked line with no lot standing at the till's location is the same. Where a location allows it, the movement posts and the trigger raises `NEGATIVE_STOCK` beside it, and also `RESERVATION_CONFLICT` (on hand below the nil reserved quantity); both are reported as raised. Whether the shop floor allows negative is the owner's (BL-083); moving what stands and excepting only the shortfall is BL-084.</li><li>**(g) Stock conflicts:** a trigger refusal becomes the exception in the movement's place by its rule: an animal sold, gone or in transit `SERIAL_CONFLICT` (URGENT); a hold covering `occurred_at`, or a lot past its expiry, `HOLD_CONFLICT` (URGENT for a withdrawal hold, REVIEW otherwise); reserved `RESERVATION_CONFLICT` (REVIEW). The exception's `details` are `{claim, fact}`: the till's shift, counter, reference, time, line and unit; the server's rule, refusal, the unit's status and place and the holds.</li><li>**(h) LOUD: a price beyond the cashier's limits, outside the band, or no longer priced is recorded, never refused, and asks for no approval.** ORD's `ck_sales_order_settled_lines` holds that a completed order has no line waiting for an approval, and its `PRICE_OVERRIDE` handler refuses a completed order, so an approval after the fact (OFFLINE_SYNC 9.6) cannot be asked for without lane B changing ORD (BL-082). The agreed price stands and its variance posts to 4210; an agreed price outside the band is recorded with the line's floor or ceiling stretched to it, the server's band kept in the exception; a line no list prices at `occurred_at` (a unit not sold, CR-038) is recorded `MANUAL_APPROVED` at the agreed price as its own band, with no entry and no variance. Each raises `PRICE_OVERRIDE` or `NOT_PRICED_AT_SYNC` (REVIEW) for a manager on the exception report.</li><li>**(h) amended 2026-11-18 (the owner's decision 3 and his clarification on prices, built in B09b continued 3, A-280 (k)):** the price the till charged always posts and the server's price is worked out only to compare; every difference is raised to the exception report's source, REVIEW within the cashier's own limit and URGENT beyond it or out of the band; beyond the limit it stands on the rules only with a supervisor's PIN on the till verified at sync and within that supervisor's own line limit (never unlimited). This replaces "recorded and raised, no approval asked" as the rule; still no approval is asked after the fact (BL-082).</li><li>**(i) Tax:** a synced sale's prices are taken as tax-inclusive, as the counter's are (A-274 (g)).</li><li>**(j) A provisional reference other than `P-<till>-<shift seq>-<counter>`** is kept in `pos.sale_sync`; the receipt carries none, and `PROVISIONAL_REF_MISMATCH` (REVIEW) is raised.</li><li>**(k) What refuses a sale (`REJECTED_INTEGRITY`):** an id that is not a UUIDv7; content that does not match its hash; an id applied before with other content; a counter of the shift another applied sale holds; a sale the catalogue cannot read (an unknown product or payment method, a unit with no conversion, a line the till's own rules forbid, a zero quantity, no lines). **The id's time window is not a refusal** (the owner on A-273 (g), which overrides OFFLINE_SYNC 4.1 steps 2 and 3, 9.8's last sentence and 11's clock row, and ADR-0013's verification line, see its Amendments): a till time, the sale's or its id's, later than the server received it by more than `sync.clock_tolerance_seconds` or earlier than its shift opened by more than it, is recorded and flagged `TILL_CLOCK_OUT_OF_TOLERANCE` (REVIEW). Tested at 300 and 301 seconds either way.</li><li>**(l) Who syncs:** the shift's cashier holding `pos.sale.create` at the till's branch and location, or a holder of `pos.shift.force_close` at the branch with a fresh step-up (the manager recovering a till); from an enrolled, unrevoked device of the shift's till whose id the batch names. A cashier whose grant was revoked while offline cannot sync; a manager can, and each such sale raises `AUTHORISATION_FAILURE` (URGENT). Every sale is attributed to the shift's cashier.</li><li>**(m) Counter gaps** are counted over every counter the shift has received, refused ones included (9.8); `pos.shift.last_device_counter` is raised while the shift is not CLOSED (a closed shift is frozen); each newly missing value is raised once (`pos.DeviceCounterGap`).</li><li>**(n) Tenders at sync:** only a live cash method allowed offline is recorded, on the shift. Any other (mobile money, card) is not recorded and raises `UNMATCHED_PAYMENT` (URGENT; REVIEW for mobile money while the branch allows it, until (a)). Tenders that do not sum to the server's receipt are recorded as the till took them and raise `TENDERS_DIFFER` (URGENT).</li><li>**(o) The order** is placed and completed at `occurred_at` (`is_offline`), its salesperson the shift's cashier.</li><li>**(p) Where exceptions go:** `pos.OfflineSaleFlagged` (one per finding), `pos.OfflineSaleRejected` and `pos.DeviceCounterGap` in the outbox, with the cashier, register, shift, device, amount, claim and fact, are the daily exception report's source (B12 builds `rpt.exception_item`). The sale's result lists every finding but a late posting for a locked period (OFFLINE_SYNC 9.4.1).</li><li>**(q) The batch:** at most 500 sales (400 `SYNC_BATCH_TOO_LARGE`, judged after the caller is authorised); bodies up to 5 MiB on `/v1/pos/sync` only.</li></ul> | Owner (a, c, e, f, h, k), Accountant (d, e, h) | B09b continued 2c | Open |

| A-283 | 2026-11-23 | **Negative stock offline, as built in B09b continued 3c (the owner's decision 2 of 2026-11-18, replacing A-278 (f); ADR-0081; ADR-0002 and ADR-0018 Amendments; migrations 0286, 0287; contract 2.22.0), each the most conservative reading.** <ul><li>**(a) Where.** An offline sale's `SALE_ISSUE_OFFLINE` may take any physical location below zero (`negative_anywhere`), countable and lot stock alike. It is rounded half-up once over the uncovered quantity. The pool covers per branch, so a location below zero inside a branch whose pool still holds stock costs at the pool's average, with no uncovered row.</li><li>**(c) Oldest first.** An arrival settles the oldest uncovered rows of its pool first (by when they were recorded, then by movement id). It appears in full only on the invoice document." Built in B09b continued 4c (contract 2.28.0, LOUD): the counter sale's answer, its stored idempotency copy and every replay show `receipt.customerTin` masked as the audit row is; the document and its archived render keep it in full; the sync result, the sync log, orders and the outbox carry it nowhere, each tested by SQL in `buyer-tin.integration.spec.ts`. The text below is the reading it replaces.] Where the TIN is written:** on the document and its archived render (E-1), and in the counter sale's stored idempotency answer for its life (the receipt as the cashier was shown it; the Cashier holds `bil.document.read`). BIL's ISSUE audit row and a refused sale's evidence carry it masked to its last four characters; no event, order or sync row carries it.</li><li>**(g) The invoice at the till:** `payInvoiceAtTill`, cash only, for an issued `INVOICE` of the till's branch, in part or whole, never beyond what is outstanding (no overpayment), change from what was handed over; on the cashier's own open shift from the till's enrolled device; online only (an invoice is not in the offline pack). The shift is judged by `tillTakesCash` (only `ON_SHIFT` is taken online, a late entry is the queue's alone) and again by PAY's `writeIn` and POS's `TillCash` under the shift's lock (ADR-0077). PAY's `recordPayment` keeps refusing cash.</li><li>**(h) Its permission:** `pos.sale.create`, the counter sale's (taking money at one's own till); no new permission. A mobile money or card payment of an invoice goes through the provider as before.</li></ul> | Owner |
| A-286 | 2026-11-23 | **Each animal's own price in the offline pack, and an animal whose weight changed between the pack and the sale, as built in B09b continued 4c (CR-052, BL-080, A-277 (f), the owner's clarification on prices of 2026-11-18, ADR-0004, ADR-0027; migration 0306; contract 2.28.0), each the most conservative reading, changeable without a contract change unless it says so.** <ul><li>**(a) What the pack prices:** every animal (a SERIAL unit) standing at the till's location that may be sold now, by MDM's `PricingService.resolveIn` exactly as the counter prices it for a buyer with no account (channel POS, the register's branch, at the pack's time), one head in the first unit its product is sold by at the till, from the animal's own `gradeId` and `lastWeightKg` as INV holds them, which are what the server prices a synced line from. Never derived from another animal or from the product's band. A lot carries no price of its own (it sells at its product's band and the scale gives the weight).</li><li>**(b) Priceless, and why:** an animal the engine cannot price has `priceBand` and `priceListEntryId` null and `unpricedReason` the engine's own reason (`MISSING_GRADE_OR_WEIGHT` for one with no weight, or no grade where its lists price by grade; `NO_RULE`; `QUOTE_ONLY`, a festival ram); an animal that cannot be sold now (reserved, on hold) is `NOT_SELLABLE`, not priced. With the seeded lists a goat with no grade is priced by its weight band alone, because the lists have such entries; a grade is needed only where a list prices by grade alone.</li><li>**(c) The weight's date and source:** `lastWeighedAt` is INV's; `lastWeightSource` is the source of LIV's latest weighing when that weighing is the weight priced (same kilograms), else null (a weight the history does not hold). POS now imports LIV, a lower layer, for `Weighing.latestIn` (additive, read only).</li><li>**(d) The difference at sync (the owner's clarification):** the price the till charged always posts; the server prices the line from the weight and grade the line carries (an animal re-weighed on the till's scale at the sale), else the animal's latest the server holds at sync, in whatever band that weight falls (CR-052's question); the difference is the line's variance on 4210 and is raised as `PRICE_OVERRIDE` by the existing rules (A-278 (h) as amended: REVIEW within the cashier's limit, URGENT beyond it or outside the band, the band stretched to the price charged). A line whose price differs and names no reason (the till charged what its pack said) is recorded under the server's new reason `OFFLINE_PACK_PRICE` (migration 0306, no note), which the pack never offers a cashier; before, such a sale was refused `REJECTED_INTEGRITY`, which broke ADR-0027 point 2 (LOUD in 2.28.0). It counts against the shift's override room like any price below the server's.</li><li>**(e) The flag's facts** add `pricedFrom` (the weight, grade and weight source the server priced an animal from). A weight recorded after the sale happened but before it synced is the server's latest and is used; the sale's own time is kept on the order.</li><li>**(f) Online,** `OFFLINE_PACK_PRICE` is an ordinary `PRICE_OVERRIDE` reason under every online rule (limits, approvals); BL-107 asks whether ORD should refuse it online. **Built in B09c (2026-11-23, BL-107):** a sale made online naming it is refused `VARIANCE_REASON_INVALID` in `sale-posting.ts`; an offline sale keeps it.</li></ul> | Owner (a, b, d), Accountant (d) | B12 | Open |
| A-287 | 2026-11-23 | **Card as an online payment, as built in B09c (FR-SHP-005, FR-PAY-003 to FR-PAY-008, FR-PAY-012, FR-PAY-013, ADR-0063, A-210, A-218; migration 0311; contract 2.30.0), each the most conservative reading, changeable as data or by a forward migration.** <ul><li>**(a) The flow:** only the shop's `placeOrder` takes a card (the parent session's leave in SHP's `checkouts.ts`, 2026-11-23). The payment is written PENDING (`UNVERIFIED`, method `CARD`, no phone) with our reference `HK-<payment id>` and its charge (channel `CARD`, no network) in the placement's serializable transaction; once it has committed, Paystack's hosted page is opened synchronously, exactly where the mobile money charge is sent (no outbox, ADR-0010): `POST /transaction/initialize` for the order's estimate in GHS from the server, channels `card` only, the farm's mailbox (`PAYSTACK_PAYER_EMAIL`). No card number, expiry or security code is ever sent to or by this API; a request naming one is 400 (unknown field). A staff-taken card payment, and a card at the counter, are not built (BL-109).</li><li>**(b) The page's address:** answered as `payment.redirectUrl` only when it is `https://checkout.paystack.com/<access code>` exactly (no port, user, query or fragment), else refused as 503 and never stored or handed out (no open redirect); kept on the charge once (`page_url`, `trg_provider_transaction_page`). The return address is `PAYSTACK_CARD_CALLBACK_URL` from configuration (https, no query), never from a request; unset, Paystack's account default.</li><li>**(c) Waiting:** Paystack answers an unpaid page as `abandoned` at once and a declined card as `failed` while the page takes another card, so a card charge answered either way waits until 30 minutes after its order's 15-minute window (`CARD_PAGE_GRACE_MS`), then fails; the order itself stops waiting with its checkout's hold, as for mobile money. A late payment within the grace clears as an unmatched deposit with a break (A-210 (c)).</li><li>**(d) A page that cannot be opened** (Paystack down, timed out, or answering another host) leaves the order PLACED and the payment PENDING and answers 503 `PAYMENT_PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`; a replay with the same Idempotency-Key opens the page, or answers the stored one, never under a new reference. A page Paystack made but whose answer was lost cannot be fetched again (a second initialise is `Duplicate Transaction Reference`): nobody holds it, so nobody can pay it, and the payment fails with its grace.</li><li>**(e) Money:** exact pesewas both ways (GH₵55.00 is sent and read back as 5,500, by the fake and the sandbox). A card clears as a deposit, Dr 1120 Payment provider clearing against Paystack, Cr 2200, by ADR-0003's existing `TENDER` determination for `CARD` (migration 0167); refunded, Cr 1120. No account added.</li><li>**(f) Abuse case 45:** a card paid after its payment failed (`PROVIDER_PAID_AFTER_FAILURE`) and a chargeback Paystack confirms by `GET /dispute/:id` (`PROVIDER_DISPUTE_OPENED`, from `charge.dispute.create`) are URGENT breaks, nothing posted, for a person to refund or answer; a dispute whose lookup cannot be made is raised marked unconfirmed (a flag moves no money); one whose lookup differs is `REJECTED_AMOUNT`. The same success after failure for mobile money is not raised (BL-108).</li><li>**(g) Webhooks:** the signature is checked over the raw bytes before the body is parsed, and a refused body keeps nothing (`payload` `{}`), for every provider payment. A replayed event is logged `REPLAYED` with no effect and does not reach the exception report: Paystack's own retries are the common case. Card details in Paystack's body (bin, last four, expiry, authorisation code, card holder) are never kept, logged or published.</li><li>**(h) The sandbox (2026-11-23):** an initialised card page verifies `abandoned` at once with `gateway_response` `The transaction was not completed` and `channel` `card`; a second initialise under the same reference is 400 `Duplicate Transaction Reference`; a refund of the unpaid page is refused; `GET /dispute` lists, and an unknown dispute is 404 `Dispute not found` (`code: not_found`). The fake matches each. A paid card, its refund and a real dispute were not run: no card number is ever sent by this suite, so they need a person to pay a page by hand (`PAYSTACK_SANDBOX_CARD_REFERENCE`), and a dispute cannot be raised in test mode from here.</li></ul> | Owner (a, c, d, f), Accountant (e, f) | B09c | **Owner, 2026-11-23 (BL-109 closed):** "the till takes cash and mobile money only for now. No card at the till. Online card payment (B09c) stays." |
| A-300 | 2026-11-17 | **Customer statements as built in B08 continued 2d (FR-WHL-009, ADR-0085 proposed; migrations 0240 to 0244; contract 2.7.0), each the most conservative reading, changeable as data or by a forward migration with no contract change unless named.** <ul><li>**(a) The figures** are the customer's 1200 against its `CUSTOMER` subledger party at one branch, read through FIN's `Journals.customerReceivablesIn` in the producing transaction, so an opening balance by manual journal, a till payment, a refund and a reversal are all on it (ADR-0085).</li><li>**(b) One branch per statement:** the branch whose 1200 lines it reads and whose series numbers it. `StatementCreate.branchId` is optional (additive); without it the caller must hold `whl.statement.create` at exactly one whole branch, else 422 `STATEMENT_INVALID` `BRANCH_REQUIRED`, as `createOrder` does.</li><li>**(c) A customer in credit** is shown as `docs/data-model/whl.md` says: a closing (or opening) balance of zero with the credit beside it in `closingCreditPesewas` (`openingCreditPesewas`), each line's running balance with `balanceSide` `C`; the rendering prints `CR` and a sentence that the credit is held for the customer. No separate deposit line is added: the credit is a figure of the header.</li><li>**(d) The kinds:** `bil.InvoiceIssued` debit is `INVOICE` (a receipt to a named customer included), `bil.DebitNoteIssued` debit `DEBIT_NOTE`, `bil.CreditNoteIssued` credit `CREDIT_NOTE`, `pay.RefundPaid` debit `REFUND`, any other `pay.*` credit `PAYMENT` and debit `PAYMENT_REVERSAL`, anything else `ADJUSTMENT`. Refunds, reversals and adjustment debits are `otherChargesPesewas`, adjustment credits `otherCreditsPesewas` (new response fields; three new `StatementLine.kind` values, additive on a response). A line is named by its source's number, else `Payment` or `Journal` and the journal's number.</li><li>**(e) Once per period:** one ISSUED statement per customer, branch and period (`uq_statement_issued_period`); asking again answers the first with 201 as it stands, even if a later posting into an earlier open date has changed the ledger since, because an issued statement is what was sent. A draft (`issue: false`) is stored with no number and no rendering, and is never issued afterwards: issuing is a new request.</li><li>**(f) Grants (migration 0244):** FR-WHL-009's actors, the Accountant and Sales, produce and read statements, and so do the Owner and the Manager, who must hold every permission of a role they grant (FR-IAM-022: the Manager grants Sales; `iam/access-admin` holds it). Neither permission needs step-up: a statement moves no money.</li><li>**(g) Lists carry no lines:** a `listStatements` row has `lines: []` and every figure; `getStatement` and `createStatement` answer every line (low-bandwidth discipline).</li><li>**(h) The period** ends today (Africa/Accra) at the latest and spans at most 366 days; the number's year is the issue date's.</li><li>**(i) Not on the statement yet:** FR-WHL-009's ageing summary (B10 builds ageing, FR-WHL-010, and adds it to the statement additively); money held on deposit for the customer (2200) or as store credit (2210), which is not on 1200 (B10 decides whether to show it). `sendStatement` stays planned for B11 (ADR-0062). A customer reads no statement: the three operations are staff-only, and a customer's own read arrives with B11's sends.</li></ul> | Accountant (a, c, d, i), Owner (b, e, f, g) | B10 | Open |

| A-313 | 2026-11-18 | **Credit control as built (B10 continued, FR-WHL-006, FR-WHL-007, CR-046, A-212 (d), ADR-0088), each the most conservative reversible reading.** (a) **Exposure is read live, never stored**, in the confirming transaction under the account's row lock: the customer's open items at every branch (issued invoices, receipts and debit notes with anything outstanding, BIL's settlement) plus its confirmed **credit** orders not yet invoiced at their estimated totals (cash-on-delivery and prepaid orders extend no credit); `business_account.exposure_pesewas` and the planned subscriptions are not built. (b) **Past the terms** is anything outstanding one day or more past its due date; a document with no due date is due on its issue date. (c) Reasons are named in the order hold, limit, terms; the refusal's code is the first. (d) **The approval request takes the place of the refusal** when `confirmOrder` carries `creditOverrideReason` (optional, additive) or through `requestCreditOverride`; without a written reason the order is refused with 422 and the amounts in words; it stays `PLACED`; once approved, `confirmOrder` is called again and confirms with `OVERRIDDEN` while the override covers the same order at the same amount, no more over the limit, for no new reason. (e) The approver holds `whl.credit.override` (Owner, Manager) with step-up and is never the asker (CR-048 in place of FR-WHL-007's "Manager or Accountant"). (f) A credit order of a customer with no account, or a closed one, is refused (`CREDIT_CHECK_UNAVAILABLE`, A-224). (g) **A bounced cheque counts** because its reversal reopens what it paid in the same transaction: the amount is outstanding again and past its terms if the invoice is; no further penalty is applied. | A-318 | 2026-11-18 | **Dunning as built (B10 continued 2, FR-WHL-011; migrations 0265 to 0267; contract 2.16.0).** (a) **Who is reminded:** invoices and debit notes still owed by a customer with a business account, on hold or not, never closed; a customer with no account is not dunned. | A-337 | 2026-11-23 | **Dispatch as built (B11 continued, FR-ORD-011, FR-DEL-003, FR-DEL-005, ADR-0091, ADR-0092, migrations 0299 to 0304).** (a) `dispatchFulfilment` takes a `PACKED` delivery order whole: every packed pick list goes on one dispatch, with a driver who drives for the branch (holds `del.proof.create` there) and a live VEHICLE location of it, both required (422 otherwise); a partial dispatch with a backorder (FR-ORD-012) is B11 continued 2's. ADR-0091's "consumes the reservations" is realised as ending them and moving the picks in the same serializable transaction, since a pick may differ from its reservation (another lot, another place, its weighed kilograms). (c) A line is fixed FULFILLED, or PARTIAL when the customer agreed to less (CR-054). | A-342 | 2026-11-23 | **Margin over the selling price as built (the owner's decision 4 of 2026-11-23, UAT scenario 2; B10 continued 6, migration 0307, contract 2.29.0, LOUD).** (a) **The sweep:** the only margin figure computed in code was QUO's `marginPercent`, worked over cost: it is markup. It keeps its value and its old contract name for one release, deprecated, beside the same figure as `markupPercent` (over cost on the product lines' net, as stored in `quo.quote_version.margin_percent`, whose comment now names it markup; the column's rename to `markup_percent` is BL-098, expand then contract). The margin is the new `marginOverPricePercent`: (net less cost) over net, the net being the lines and the delivery fee before tax, as UAT scenario 2 reads it (30.75 for 1,906,000 over 1,320,000; 30.77 for 1,716,000 over 1,188,000), derived on read from the stored net and cost, never stored (ADR-0001), half-up once to two places (half away from zero below cost), never above 100, null with no cost known or no net. CST's planned `MarginRow.marginPercent` was already over revenue (its examples check: 30.88 is 2,730,000 over 8,840,000): only its description was added. PUR's order-splitting "margin" is a closeness band, not a profit figure, and is untouched. (b) **The floor** (setting `quo.margin_floor_basis_points`, no seeded or stored row: the code's default 1,000 applies) is now read as a margin over the selling price, in basis points of the price, compared exactly: (price less cost) x 10,000 < floor x price. It was read over cost; the stored number is not converted. The same number over price is always at least as strict (for a gain, margin is below markup), so **every quote that passed the old check either passes the new one or now needs approval**, and none that failed it now passes: 1,570,800 over 1,428,000 (10.00 percent markup, at the old floor) now waits, tested. (c) **The floor is judged on the product lines alone**, without the delivery fee, as before (A-309 (d)), because the fee carries a delivery cost the estimate does not hold: a fee never lifts a version over the floor, so the floor's figure can sit a little below the margin shown (UAT 2.2: 30.53 judged, 30.75 shown, both clear of 20 percent). The more conservative of the two. (d) The approval's summary names the margin over the price. | Owner | B10 continued 6 | Open |

(b) **The match:** before the decision's transaction, at read committed (no predicate lock on IAM's sessions), QUO asks IAM's one new read, `StaffSessionsSeen.fromAddressIn` (the parent session's leave; no IAM column was needed), for the staff realm's sessions **created from the decision's address** (`iam.session.ip_created`, the only address IAM records) and **last seen no earlier than 24 hours before**, by the application clock (ADR-0067), the edge inclusive, revoked or not, created no later than the decision. An address that is not an IP matches nothing. (c) **The browser:** a match from the same address in **another browser is still flagged**, marked `sameBrowser: false` (it flags and never blocks, so the more visible reading is the conservative one); the same browser is an exact match of the user agent on its first 500 characters, and two unknown user agents are never the same browser. (d) **The flag:** each match is a row of `quo.quote_link_staff_match` (LEDGER) and the decision publishes `quo.QuoteLinkDecidedFromStaffAddress` v1 (the staff, each with `sameBrowser`, the quote, the link, the decision, `abuseCase` 40, `windowHours` 24; no address, user agent, session id or token), replacing `quo.QuoteLinkDecidedInStaffBrowser`, which is no longer published; B12's exception kind stays `QUOTE_ACCEPTED_BY_STAFF` (CR-059's name). The buyer's decision always stands. (e) **Residual:** a session that moved address after it was created is not matched (IAM records no later address; recording one would change IAM's authentication path, lane A's); a shared address flags a buyer deciding beside staff, which the browser match helps a reviewer tell apart. | Owner | B10 continued 6 | Open |

| A-347 | 2026-11-23 | **A driver's run as built (B11 continued 3, FR-DEL-005, FR-DEL-007, FR-DEL-011, CR-056 items 3 and 4, CR-057 point 2, ADR-0092, the owner's close rule of 2026-11-23; migrations 0318 to 0320).** (a) **A run is one driver, one vehicle, one branch, one day** (`del.route`, UNIQUE driver and date as the dictionary has it: one run per driver per day; a second run the same day is BL-099). Its stops are deliveries dispatch already loaded onto that vehicle for that driver (`ASSIGNED`), at that branch, on no other run, dispatched on or before its date, in the order the Manager gives (no optimisation, BL-033); stops are set and reordered only while it is `PLANNED`, never added or dropped once planned (reassignment is B11 continued 4's). A date in the past is refused. (b) **Starting** (`startRoute`, on or after its day, never before) moves nothing: dispatch loaded the van (ADR-0092 replaces FR-DEL-005's "starting loads the stock"). Until its run has started, a stop on it is not taken on the way, delivered or failed (`ROUTE_NOT_STARTED`, and `ck_delivery_route` in the database); a delivery on no run is not held back (as built in B11 continued). (c) **Closing** (`completeRoute`, new): refused while any stop's goods are on the van (only `DELIVERED` and `RETURNED` count as off it; the write-off with a reason joins them in B11 continued 4, with the unaccounted on the driver variance report) or while any collection made on the run is still held by the driver; the database holds the same at commit (`ck_route_closed`). A hand-in still takes everything the driver holds at the branch (A-345 (b)), so one hand-in may cover two runs. (e) **On the way** records the time once and queues `del.DeliveryOnTheWay` (NTF sends it, B11 continued 4); a stop already on its way answers unchanged. **A failed attempt** (`recordFailedDelivery`) is insert-only with a `DELIVERY_FAILURE` reason, the next action asked and the device's id (a resend answers the first); the delivery is `FAILED`, the order `DELIVERY_FAILED` (ORD on `del.DeliveryFailed`), and the goods stay on the van. (f) **`getMyRoute` with no run that day is 404** (CR-057 point 2). (g) **The drivers' list** (`listDrivers`, `del.route.manage`) is everyone who may be given a delivery at the branch as dispatch judges it (holds `del.proof.create` there, A-337 (d)), so the Manager and the Owner appear beside the Drivers. (h) **Grants:** `del.route.read` and `del.route.manage` to the Manager and the Owner only. | A-360 | 2026-11-23 | **The van's two other ways out as built (FR-DEL-011; the owner's van rule of 2026-11-23; B11 continued 4, migrations 0321 to 0325, contract 2.33.0, ADR-0092).** (a) **Only a FAILED delivery** (its order `DELIVERY_FAILED`, its goods still the farm's on the vehicle) takes a way out other than delivery; a stop still assigned or on its way first has its failed attempt recorded by its driver. Both ways out are the Manager's and the Owner's (`del.delivery.return`, `del.delivery.write_off`, new), never the Driver's. (b) **Returned to stock** (`returnDeliveryToStock`, ORD's): each dispatched pick moved `TRANSFER_INTERNAL` from the vehicle into the named live `RETURNS_HOLD` of the delivery's branch, sourced on the delivery; not sellable until inspected because that location kind is never promised online nor picked from (as built); the delivery `RETURNED`; the order `RETURNED`. (c) **Written off** (`writeOffDelivery`, new, ORD's): INV's own adjustment at the vehicle (each pick a loss valued at cost, never a figure from the request) with a `WASTAGE` reason or the new `UNACCOUNTED_ON_VAN` (a note needed), through INV's `STOCK_WRITE_OFF` policy: below the threshold it posts at once; at or above it the goods stay on the van (the run cannot close) until a different holder of `inv.adjustment.approve` approves, when the delivery is `WRITTEN_OFF` in that approval's transaction (ORD reads IAM's `iam.ApprovalDecided`); refused, the way out is `REJECTED`, the goods still aboard, and it may be asked again. `WRITTEN_OFF` is a new posted delivery status with its reason, value and adjustment (`ck_delivery_written_off`); the order is `RETURNED` (the order machine has no written-off status; `RETURNED` then `CANCELLED` is its end). A way out never takes the van below zero (INV's trigger, `INSUFFICIENT_STOCK`). (d) **The money:** the customer is not charged for goods never received, so the **whole document** is credited, the delivery fee included (no delivery was made), reason `DELIVERY_RETURNED` (new), through BIL's `CREDIT_NOTE` approval by someone other than the Manager (BIL's new `requestWholeCreditIn`, `issueCreditNote`'s path in the caller's transaction). Once that credit note is issued, what the customer paid beyond what the document still asks (`planReturnRefund`) is asked back through PAY's `REFUND` approval against it, the Manager its maker, to the original method, or to store credit only for a customer account (a guest 422 `STORE_CREDIT_NEEDS_CUSTOMER`, since PAY would refuse it at the approval). A refused credit note leaves the document as it is; staff may raise one through `issueCreditNote`. The conservative reading: no money moves without its own approval by a second person. (e) **The driver variance report's goods line** (`listDriverGoodsVariances`, new, under `del.cash_reconciliation.read`): only goods written off as `UNACCOUNTED_ON_VAN`, at the adjustment's value at cost, by driver and day; spoiled or damaged goods are wastage, not the driver's variance. `del.DeliveryWrittenOff` names `unaccounted` and abuse case 48 for B12's exception report. (f) **Residual:** the CREDIT_NOTE policy asks for approval at every amount (A-200), so a write-off approved later never issues a credit note at once inside IAM's decision (which would need the credit-note series lock); if the policy ever lets small credits through, revisit. | Owner | B11 continued 5 | Open |

## Open: settings and definitions introduced by the SRS

Written in B1 with `docs/SRS.md` section 7.4, which cites them by these ids. Each is a setting or a definition: changing it changes data, not code.

| SA-02 | 2026-11-13 | A bulk export is more than 1,000 rows and needs step-up (as the contract's `rpt.report.export.bulk` permission states) (used by FR-MDM-020, FR-RPT-005, SEC-009) | Owner | B4 | Open |
| SA-03 | 2026-11-13 | Webhook timestamp window of 5 minutes either side (used by FR-PAY-004, SEC-005) | Backend lead | B9a | Open |
| SA-04 | 2026-11-13 | Signed URLs for private files live 15 minutes for confidential files and 5 minutes for restricted ones (`docs/security/DATA_CLASSIFICATION.md`, A-050) (used by FR-DOC-007) | Backend lead | B8 | Open |
| SA-05 | 2026-11-13 | An account goes on hold when any invoice is more than 30 days past due (used by FR-WHL-008) | Owner | B10 | Open |
| SA-06 | 2026-11-13 | Quote validity 14 days; margin floor 10 percent over cost (used by FR-QUO-004, FR-QUO-005) | Owner | B10 | Open. **Amended 2026-11-23 (the owner, A-342):** the floor is a margin over the selling price, 10 percent of the price. |
| SA-07 | 2026-11-13 | Yield variance flag when actual differs from expected by more than 5 percent of expected (used by FR-PRC-005) | Owner | B6 | Open |
| SA-08 | 2026-11-13 | Three-way match tolerance: price 1 percent, quantity zero beyond the receipt (used by FR-PUR-009) | Accountant | B7 | Open |
| SA-09 | 2026-11-13 | Quiet hours 21:00 to 07:00 Africa/Accra; security codes and delivery-day messages exempt (used by FR-NTF-006) | Owner | B11 | Open |
| SA-10 | 2026-11-13 | Webhook delivery: 8 attempts, exponential backoff from 1 minute with jitter, capped at 6 hours (used by FR-API-009) | Backend lead | B12 | Open |
| SA-11 | 2026-11-13 | Upload caps: images 5 MB; PDF and spreadsheets 10 MB (used by FR-DOC-006) | Backend lead | B8 | Open |

## Notes for the frontend track

These follow from backend decisions and affect what the frontend builds. They are repeated in the B0 session report.

| ID | Note |
| --- | --- |
| F-001 | Session cookies are bound to the API host. Storefront pages that need the customer's identity fetch that data in the browser; server-rendered catalogue pages are anonymous (ADR-0016) |
| F-002 | Every state-changing request sends an `Idempotency-Key` and an `X-CSRF-Token` (ADR-0014, ADR-0016) |
| F-003 | Offline sales carry a client-generated UUIDv7, a device counter and a provisional reference, and are cash only by default (ADR-0015, ADR-0027) |
| F-004 | Checkout must show the estimated total, the maximum total and the final-charge rule for catch-weight items (ADR-0021) |
| F-005 | A product in `REQUEST_TO_BUY` mode opens a request for quote instead of adding to the cart (ADR-0019) |
| F-006 | A price that does not resolve is a "quote required" result, not an error (ADR-0004) |
| F-007 | Slot 10 is two backend sessions, B9a then B9b. F9 is unchanged (ADR-0026) |
| A-362 | 2026-11-23 | **A delivery's driver, vehicle and slot after dispatch as built (B11 continued 5, FR-DEL-001 to FR-DEL-004, FR-DEL-011; migration 0326, contract 2.34.0).** (a) **Reassignment** (`assignDelivery`, `del.delivery.assign`, the Manager's and the Owner's): only a delivery waiting on the van (`ASSIGNED`) or back from a failed attempt (`FAILED`), never one on its way or finished, never a stop of a `PLANNED` or `IN_PROGRESS` run (it keeps its driver and van, `ck_delivery_route`; 422 `ON_A_RUN`), never while its write-off waits for approval (`WRITE_OFF_PENDING`, the adjustment names the first van); a driver who drives for the branch and a live vehicle of it; the same driver and vehicle 422 `UNCHANGED`. When the vehicle changes ORD moves each dispatched pick from the one van to the other (`TRANSFER_INTERNAL`, still the farm's) in the same transaction, refused below zero; the old driver can no longer open the delivery. Audited before and after; `del.DeliveryReassigned` in the outbox. (b) **Booking** (`rescheduleDelivery`): a live slot of the delivery's own zone, read under its row lock, never more bookings than its capacity; a slot that has started (to the minute) is refused; today's slots are offered only up to the zone's cut-off, Africa/Accra, the cut-off minute itself still before it (14:00:00 books today, 14:00:01 does not); the old slot freed and the new taken in one statement. **The counts are the database's:** a delivery naming a slot holds one unit while it is not `CANCELLED`, `RETURNED` or `WRITTEN_OFF` (a `DELIVERED` one keeps it: the slot was used), `booked` moves only through a delivery's booking (`ck_delivery_slot_booked_by_delivery`), and `ck_delivery_slot_booked` refuses an overbooking whoever writes. (c) **The reattempt:** rescheduling a `FAILED` delivery sends it back to `ASSIGNED` and its order from `DELIVERY_FAILED` to `DISPATCHED` (ORD on `del.DeliveryRescheduled`, in the same transaction); the same slot may be taken again for a reattempt without a second unit; the same slot for a delivery waiting on the van 422 `SAME_SLOT`. (d) **A stop of a live run is booked only on its run's day** (`NOT_ON_RUN_DAY`): its stop and van stay with that run (`del.route_stop` never moves) and the run cannot close with its goods aboard, so a failed stop reattempted another day waits on its van and its run stays open until then; moving a failed stop to a later run is BL-110. (e) Dispatch still makes the delivery with no slot (the order does not keep the checkout's slot); the slot is booked by `rescheduleDelivery`; carrying the checkout's chosen slot onto the delivery at dispatch is B11 continued 6's. (f) `del.DeliveryRescheduled` names no phone or name; NTF tells the customer (FR-DEL-003) in its own sub-domain. | Owner | B11 continued 5 | Open |

| A-364 | 2026-11-23 | **The van's last ways and the driver's queue as built (B11 continued 6, FR-ORD-014, FR-DEL-008, FR-DEL-009, FR-DEL-011, CR-055, CR-057 point 3, CR-063; migrations 0327 and 0328, contract 2.35.0, ADR-0093).** (a) **Cancelling a dispatched delivery** (`cancelDelivery`, `del.delivery.assign`, as the contract planned it): only one whose goods still wait on the van (`SCHEDULED` or `ASSIGNED`) on no `PLANNED` or `IN_PROGRESS` run (`ON_A_RUN`); one on its way is the driver's to deliver or fail, a `FAILED` one goes back through `returnDeliveryToStock`. FR-ORD-014 allows no cancel after dispatch but the delivery return and the order machine has no `DISPATCHED` to `CANCELLED`, so the cancellation **is** the delivery return along the machine's own edges: the delivery `CANCELLED` with a `CANCELLATION` reason (`cancel_reason_code`, `cancelled_at`, `ck_delivery_cancelled`); each pick from the van into a live `RETURNS_HOLD` the Manager names (not sellable until inspected, even though the van never left: the conservative choice); the order `DISPATCHED` to `DELIVERY_FAILED` to `RETURNED`, each history row naming the cancellation; the whole document credited (`DELIVERY_RETURNED`) and the refund (`ORDER_RETURNED`) as `returnDeliveryToStock`'s, recorded as its way out `RETURN_TO_STOCK`. No new order edge; the order stays `RETURNED` (its close-out to `CANCELLED` once credited and refunded is BL-112, as for every van return). (b) **A part delivery** (CR-055, ADR-0093): the proof's `lines` say what was kept of each line in its own unit, a line not named kept whole; never more than dispatched; a weighed line (contents with a weight, a catch-weight pack) kept or returned **whole**, so one pack of two weighed packs cannot be returned at the door (BL-113); nothing kept is refused (`NOTHING_DELIVERED`, a failed attempt instead); a short line needs a `DELIVERY_FAILURE` reason and its note where the reason asks (`returnedNote`, new), a reason refused when all was kept. What was kept is issued for the sale from the van, the rest moves from the van **into the branch's returns hold at once** (CR-055's "returns the rest to stock"; keeping it on the van until a Manager checks it in is BL-111), the last picks' parts first, in whole base units (`UNIT_SPLIT` otherwise). BIL prices the credit at the door (its own preview; the delivery fee never credited); the driver collects at most the cash due less it, never below zero (`ck_proof_variance` now against `greatest(due - credit, 0)`); the cash due itself never changes. The credit note is asked through the `CREDIT_NOTE` approval **with the driver as its maker** (a different person approves); what a paid-ahead customer paid beyond what they kept is refunded to the original method once it is issued. The order moves `DELIVERED` as for any proof and does not complete until settled (BL-097, B11 continued 7). (c) **The driver's queue** (`syncDriverEvents`, `del.proof.create`): each record in its own serializable transaction by the online rules, the caller's own deliveries only; exactly once by its client id, which must be a UUIDv7 (`CLIENT_ID_NOT_V7` otherwise); a resend answers `DUPLICATE` with `firstOutcome` and the first `reason`, derived from the stored proof or attempt (`COD_VARIANCE:<reason>`, `PART_DELIVERY:<reason>`); a refusal is `REJECTED` with its codes and writes nothing; an id used for another delivery `ID_IN_USE`. No clock-window check beyond the proof's own rules (a phone's capture time before dispatch is refused as online). (d) **CR-063** (`listShiftDriverCashHolders`, with the parent session's leave, additive in POS): the hand-in's own shift check, each driver holding a collection at the shift's branch not handed in and how many, never an amount; the calling cashier is never listed. | Owner | B11 continued 6 | Open |

An approved override covers it when the gross is no more than it was approved for and no more over the limit (`overrideCoversDispatch`); otherwise refused with nothing written, or with `creditOverrideReason` the override is asked for that gross (202). | A-372 | 2026-11-24 | **Part of a crate returned as single eggs** (the owner, 2026-11-18). (a) A return line may name `uom`, the product's base unit, on a line sold per unit in a unit of several (an egg of a crate, from MDM's exact whole conversion, kept on the line as `pieces_per_unit`); any other unit is `UNIT_NOT_SOLD`; whole pieces only. (b) Its refund is the credit note's amount on the original line, the running total of ADR-0075 on the line's anchor on the original document (its gross when tax-inclusive, its net when not, then taxed by the original's scheme as every credit): round(paid x (previous + returning) / pieces sold) less round(paid x previous / pieces sold), where previous counts every decided return of the line in pieces (whole units times the count). Whole units keep the quantity credit as before. (c) **Conservative:** a return in single pieces is refused while another return of its line is open, and a line with one open takes no other (`RETURN_OPEN_ON_LINE`, held by the database too), so the refund an approver sees is the one paid and the pieces always add up; a return of whole crates beside whole crates may still wait together as before. (d) Single pieces go to the returns hold or are written off, never `RESTOCK` (`PART_RETURN_TO_HOLD`): inspected first. (e) A line whose pieces round to nothing (a line that cost under one pesewa a piece) is refused by BIL's amount rule; none is priced so. (f) On a tax-exclusive invoice the last piece's credit may meet BIL's cap one pesewa early if the credit taxes round up; eggs are VAT-exempt and none is seeded so. | Owner | B11 continued 9 | Open |
